1698 — forward hops

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1698 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Short, springy hops forward that build leg power, elasticity and coordination.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand on both feet with your knees slightly bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1698.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand on both feet with your knees slightly bent. 1698.json#/instructions/0
  2. Swing your arms and hop forward a short distance. 1698.json#/instructions/1
  3. Land softly on the balls of your feet. 1698.json#/instructions/2
  4. Reset your posture and hop again. 1698.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hops controlled and your chest tall. 1698.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1699 after decompression) · Source record

A powerful rotational throw that trains the core, hips and shoulders to work together.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand sideways to a wall holding a medicine ball at your hip. 1699.json#/instructions/0
  2. Rotate away from the wall and load your back hip. 1699.json#/instructions/1
  3. Explosively rotate and throw the ball into the wall. 1699.json#/instructions/2
  4. Catch it on the rebound and repeat. 1699.json#/instructions/3
  5. Switch sides after the planned reps. 1699.json#/instructions/4

1700 — dumbbell push press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1700 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An overhead dumbbell press that uses a small leg dip and drive for momentum. It builds the shoulders and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand at shoulder level.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1700.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand at shoulder level. 1700.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and dip your body down, then explosively extend your legs and press the dumbbells overhead. 1700.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lock out your arms at the top of the movement, then lower the dumbbells back to shoulder level. 1700.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1700.json#/instructions/3

1701 — medicine ball close grip push up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1701 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A close grip push-up with your hands on a medicine ball. It hits the triceps and challenges stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start in a high plank position with your hands on the medicine ball, shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1701.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Start in a high plank position with your hands on the medicine ball, shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1701.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use toe support under the documented support leg or legs and keep the pelvis level; when a lifted-leg source leaves the first side open, stage the right leg first and then mirror.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1701.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Medicine ball. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1701.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a high plank position with your hands on the medicine ball, shoulder-width apart. 1701.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lower your body towards the ground by bending your elbows, keeping them close to your sides. 1701.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push back up to the starting position, fully extending your arms. 1701.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1701.json#/instructions/3

1702 — single leg foot touch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1702 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg balance drill where you reach down to touch the floor or foot, training hip control and stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand on one leg with a soft knee. 1702.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge at the hips and reach down toward your foot or the floor. 1702.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back flat and your free leg extended behind you. 1702.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return to standing by squeezing your glute. 1702.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish all reps, then switch legs. 1702.json#/instructions/4

1705 — squat on bosu ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1705 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat on a bosu ball. It trains the quads and glutes and tests balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Place the bosu ball on the ground with the flat side up. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and position yourself on top of the bosu ball.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1705.json#/instructions/0, 1705.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1705.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and position yourself on top of the bosu ball.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1705.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: BOSU half ball. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1705.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place the bosu ball on the ground with the flat side up. 1705.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and position yourself on top of the bosu ball. 1705.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your body down by bending your knees and hips, as if sitting back into a chair. 1705.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your chest up and your weight on your heels. 1705.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower yourself until your thighs are parallel to the ground. 1705.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment, then push through your heels to return to the starting position. 1705.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1705.json#/instructions/6

  • Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and position yourself on top of the bosu ball. Step 2; 1705.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

  • Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and position yourself on top of the bosu ball. Step 2; 1705.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

  • Lower your body down by bending your knees and hips, as if sitting back into a chair. Step 3; 1705.json#/instructions/2
  • Keep your chest up and your weight on your heels. Step 4; 1705.json#/instructions/3
  • Lower yourself until your thighs are parallel to the ground. Step 5; 1705.json#/instructions/4

1731 — dumbbell close grip press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1731 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A close grip dumbbell press. It builds the triceps along with the chest.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a flat bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1731.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Sit on a flat bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1731.json#/instructions/0

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1731.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench, Flat bench configuration. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1731.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a flat bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs. 1731.json#/instructions/0
  2. Using your thighs to help raise the dumbbells, lift the dumbbells one at a time so that you can hold them in front of you at shoulder width. 1731.json#/instructions/1
  3. Once at shoulder width, rotate your wrists forward so that the palms of your hands are facing away from you. This will be your starting position. 1731.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you breathe in, slowly lower the dumbbells to your side until they are about level with your chest. 1731.json#/instructions/3
  5. As you exhale, use your triceps to lift the dumbbells back to the starting position. Make sure to use only your triceps and do not use your forearms or biceps to help lift the dumbbells. 1731.json#/instructions/4
  6. After a second pause at the contracted position, repeat the movement for the prescribed amount of repetitions. 1731.json#/instructions/5

  • Once at shoulder width, rotate your wrists forward so that the palms of your hands are facing away from you. This will be your starting position. Step 3; 1731.json#/instructions/2
  • As you exhale, use your triceps to lift the dumbbells back to the starting position. Make sure to use only your triceps and do not use your forearms or biceps to help lift the dumbbells. Step 5; 1731.json#/instructions/4

1743 — dumbbell twisting bench press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1743 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A dumbbell bench press with a wrist twist through the rep. Works chest and triceps while adding a rotating element.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie flat on a bench with your feet flat on the ground and your back pressed against the bench. Hold a dumbbell in each hand with an overhand grip, palms facing away from you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1743.json#/instructions/0, 1743.json#/instructions/1

Hold a dumbbell in each hand with an overhand grip, palms facing away from you.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1743.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie flat on a bench with your feet flat on the ground and your back pressed against the bench. 1743.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold a dumbbell in each hand with an overhand grip, palms facing away from you. 1743.json#/instructions/1
  3. Extend your arms straight up over your chest, keeping a slight bend in your elbows. 1743.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower the dumbbells down towards your chest, keeping your elbows close to your body. 1743.json#/instructions/3
  5. As you lower the dumbbells, twist your wrists so that your palms face towards you at the bottom of the movement. 1743.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then reverse the movement by pressing the dumbbells back up to the starting position. 1743.json#/instructions/5
  7. As you press the dumbbells up, twist your wrists back to the starting position with palms facing away from you. 1743.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1743.json#/instructions/7

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1766 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: prone.

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with your arms overhead and your legs straight. 1798.json#/instructions/0
  2. Reach your fingertips up and your toes down, as if you want to get taller. 1798.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1798.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds without bouncing. 1798.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release gently and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one-sided. 1798.json#/instructions/4
